
Category: Cybersecurity
Importance: 8
A verified VS Code extension for Anthropic's AI model was compromised, affecting approximately 5,000 developers due to a supply-chain attack. The malicious version was available for download for nearly 12 hours before being removed, and it attempted to install a persistent backdoor allowing attackers potential access to sensitive information on users' machines.

 - A supply-chain attack compromised a verified VS Code extension.
 - Around 5,000 developers may have been affected by the backdoored version.

Source: https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/10/anthropic-vs-code-extension-on-cursor-backdoored-for-nearly-12-hours/

 - A backdoored version of a widely used VS Code extension was distributed to developers.
 - The attackers potentially accessed sensitive user data and could conduct follow-up attacks.

Cursor experienced two significant security incidents involving its integrated development environment (IDE) on October 29, 2026. One incident involved the hijacking of Anthropic's Claude Code extension, which introduced a malicious version that could steal sensitive data and potentially grant unauthorized access, affecting around 5,000 users. The second vulnerability allowed attackers to execute arbitrary code on developers' machines through Cursor's built-in browser, raising serious security implications for users and the software development environment.

 - Cursor faced a security breach with the hijacking of its Claude Code extension, which was exploited to steal user data.
 - An unrelated vulnerability allowed attackers to deploy arbitrary code to developers' machines via Cursor's built-in browser.

On October 29, 2026, a malicious version of the Claude Code extension was identified in the Cursor extension marketplace, which attempted a supply-chain attack, allowing unauthorized remote access to users' systems. The incident involved a backdoor that could leverage SSH for persistent access and affected systems were advised to remove the compromised extension and follow recovery protocols.

 - A tampered copy of the Claude Code extension was available for a limited time on the Cursor marketplace.
 - The malware attempted to create persistent unauthorized access through SSH tunneling.
 - Organizations are advised to investigate and remove malicious artifacts related to the compromised extension.

Source: https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/security/blog/?p=150709

His story reflects the larger trend of immigrant contributions to American gastronomy.

 - Ranji Smile opened one of the first Indian restaurants in the US, called Omar Khayyam, in 1901.
 - Smile's innovative approach to Indian cuisine and theatrical presentations initially garnered attention but later led to his downfall.
 - His establishment paved the way for future Indian restaurants in the United States, despite his own restaurant closing within a year.

Source: https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/crm941n0413o?at_medium=RSS&at_campaign=rss
